                \texttt{You have attempted this same task before and did not succeed. These are the plans you wrote after those attempts.} \\
\texttt{Trial \#1: New plan: I failed because I got stuck in an infinite loop of dropping and picking up items in the pantry and kitchen without ever actually brewing the potion. I collected the ingredients (water, spider egg, sandwich, eye of newt) and the tools (fire wand, ice wand) but never executed the recipe steps in the kitchen. My new strategy is to stop shuttling items back and forth. Instead, I will go to the kitchen, ensure the cauldron is ready, and execute the recipe steps sequentially: pour water, swirl fire wand, add sandwich, wave ice wand, add eye of newt, and add spider egg. I will only pick up an item if it is immediately needed for the next step in the recipe, dropping it into the cauldron right away to free up inventory space, rather than trying to carry multiple ingredients at once. Once the final ingredient is added, I will immediately type "> done".} \\
\\ 
\\

I also wasted time searching for the spider egg in the pantry and magic workshop, missing the fact that it was likely in the conservatory (where the prickly pear was) or the garden. My new strategy is to strictly follow the recipe: Water, Spider Egg, Sandwich, Eye of Newt. I will first locate the spider egg by checking the conservatory and garden before starting the brewing process. Once I have the spider egg, I will execute the steps in the exact order: 1) Pour water, 2) Swirl fire wand, 3) Add sandwich, 4) Wave ice wand, 5) Add eye of newt, 6) Add spider egg.
